[codex] [codex] # GitHub Dependency Graph Gradle Plugin
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] A Gradle plugin for generating a GitHub dependency graph for a Gradle build, which can be uploaded to the [GitHub Dependency Submission API](https://docs.github.com/en/rest/dependency-graph/dependency-submission).
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] ## Usage
[codex] [codex] This plugin is designed to be used in a GitHub Actions workflow, and is tightly integrated into the 
[codex] [codex] [gradle/actions/dependency-submission](https://github.com/gradle/actions/tree/main/dependency-submission) action.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] For other uses, the [core plugin](https://plugins.gradle.org/plugin/org.gradle.github-dependency-graph-gradle-plugin) (`org.gradle.github.GitHubDependencyGraphPlugin`) 
[codex] [codex] should be applied to the `Gradle` instance via a Gradle init script as follows:
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] ```groovy
[codex] [codex] import org.gradle.github.GitHubDependencyGraphPlugin
[codex] [codex] initscript {
[codex] [codex]   repositories {
[codex] [codex]     maven {
[codex] [codex]       url = uri("https://plugins.gradle.org/m2/")
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex]   }
[codex] [codex]   dependencies {
[codex] [codex]     classpath("org.gradle:github-dependency-graph-gradle-plugin:+")
[codex] [codex]   }
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] apply plugin: GitHubDependencyGraphPlugin
[codex] [codex] ```
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] This causes 2 separate plugins to be applied, that can be used independently:
[codex] [codex] - `GitHubDependencyExtractorPlugin` collects all dependencies that are resolved during a build execution and writes these to a file. The output file can be found at `&lt;root>/build/reports/github-depenency-graph-snapshots/&lt;job-correlator>.json`.
[codex] [codex] - `ForceDependencyResolutionPlugin` creates a `ForceDependencyResolutionPlugin_resolveAllDependencies` task that will attempt to resolve all dependencies for a Gradle build, by simply invoking `dependencies` on all projects.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] ### Environment variables
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] The following environment variables configure the snapshot generated by the `GitHubDependencyExtractorPlugin`. See the [GitHub Dependency Submission API docs](https://docs.github.com/en/rest/dependency-graph/dependency-submission?apiVersion=2022-11-28) for details:
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] #### Required environment variables
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] - `GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_JOB_CORRELATOR`: Sets the `job.correlator` value for the dependency submission
[codex] [codex] - `GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_JOB_ID`: Sets the `job.id` value for the dependency submission
[codex] [codex] - `GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_REF`: Sets the `ref` value for the commit that generated the dependency graph
[codex] [codex] - `GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_SHA`: Sets the `sha` value for the commit that generated the dependency graph
[codex] [codex] - `GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_WORKSPACE`: Sets the root directory of the github repository. Must be an absolute path.
[codex] [codex] - `DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_REPORT_DIR` (optional): Specifies where the dependency graph report will be generated. Must be an absolute path.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] #### Optional environment variables
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] - `GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_DETECTOR_NAME`: Sets the `detector.name` value for the dependency submission. Defaults to `GitHub Dependency Graph Gradle Plugin`
[codex] [codex] - `GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_DETECTOR_VERSION`: Sets the `detector.version` value for the dependency submission. Defaults to current version of the plugin.
[codex] [codex] - `GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_DETECTOR_URL`: Sets the `detector.url` value for the dependency submission. Defaults to `https://github.com/gradle/github-dependency-graph-gradle-plugin`
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] Each of these values can also be provided via a system property. 
[codex] [codex] eg: Env var `DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_REPORT_DIR` can be set with `-DDEPENDENCY_GRAPH_REPORT_DIR=...` on the command-line.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] ### Filtering which Gradle Configurations contribute to the dependency graph
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] If you do not want to include every dependency configuration in every project in your build, you can limit the
[codex] [codex] dependency extraction to a subset of these.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] The following parameters control the set of projects and configurations that contribute dependencies.
[codex] [codex] Each of these is a regular expression value, and can set either as an environment variable or as a system property on the command line.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] | Property                                | Description               | Default                         |
[codex] [codex] |-----------------------------------------|---------------------------|---------------------------------|
[codex] [codex] | DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_INCLUDE_PROJECTS       | Projects to include       | All projects are included       |
[codex] [codex] | DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_EXCLUDE_PROJECTS       | Projects to exclude       | No projects are excluded        |
[codex] [codex] | DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_INCLUDE_CONFIGURATIONS | Configurations to include | All configurations are included |
[codex] [codex] | DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_EXCLUDE_CONFIGURATIONS | Configurations to exclude | No configurations are excluded  |
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] ### Controlling the scope of dependencies in the dependency graph
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] The GitHub dependency graph allows a scope to be assigned to each reported dependency.
[codex] [codex] The only permissible values for scope are 'runtime' and 'development'.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] The following parameters control the set of projects and configurations that provide 'runtime' scoped dependencies.
[codex] [codex] Any dependency resolution that does not match these parameters will be scoped 'development'.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] Each of these parameters is a regular expression value, and can set either as an environment variable or as a system property on the command line.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] | Property                                        | Description                                               | Default                         |
[codex] [codex] |-------------------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------|---------------------------------|
[codex] [codex] | DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_RUNTIME_INCLUDE_PROJECTS       | Projects that can provide 'runtime' dependencies          | All projects are included       |
[codex] [codex] | DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_RUNTIME_EXCLUDE_PROJECTS       | Projects that do not provide 'runtime' dependencies       | No projects are excluded        |
[codex] [codex] | DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_RUNTIME_INCLUDE_CONFIGURATIONS | Configurations that contain 'runtime' dependencies        | All configurations are included |
[codex] [codex] | DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_RUNTIME_EXCLUDE_CONFIGURATIONS | Configurations that do not contain 'runtime' dependencies | No configurations are excluded  |
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] By default, no scope is assigned to dependencies in the graph. To enable scopes in the generated dependency graph,
[codex] [codex] at least one of these parameters must be configured.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] For dependencies that are resolved in multiple projects and/or multiple configurations, only a single 'runtime' scoped resolution
[codex] [codex] is required for that dependency to be scoped 'runtime'.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] ### Gradle compatibility
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] The plugin is compatible with most versions of Gradle >= 5.2, and all changes are tested against a range of versions.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] The plugin is compatible with running Gradle with the configuration-cache enabled: this support is
[codex] [codex] limited to Gradle "8.1.0" and later. Earlier Gradle versions will not work with `--configuration-cache`.
[codex] [codex] Note that no dependency graph will be generated when configuration state is loaded from the configuration-cache.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] | Gradle version | Compatible | Compatible with configuration-cache |
[codex] [codex] | -------------- | ------- | ------------------------ |
[codex] [codex] | 1.x - 4.x      | :x: | :x: |
[codex] [codex] | 5.0 - 5.1.1 | :x: | :x: |
[codex] [codex] | 5.2 - 5.6.4 | ✅ | :x: |
[codex] [codex] | 6.0 - 6.9.4 | ✅ | :x: |
[codex] [codex] | 7.0 - 7.0.2 | :x: | :x: |
[codex] [codex] | 7.1 - 7.6.6 | ✅ | :x: |
[codex] [codex] | 8.0 - 8.0.2 | ✅ | :x: |
[codex] [codex] | 8.1 - 8.14.4 | ✅ | ✅ |
[codex] [codex] | 9.0.0 - 9.4.1 | ✅ | ✅ |
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] ### Dependency verification
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] When using this plugin with [dependency signature verification enabled](https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/dependency_verification.html#sec:signature-verification), 
[codex] [codex] the you should be able to update your `dependency-verification.xml` file using `--write-verification-metadata pgp,sha256`.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] However, if this doesn't work, you can add the following to your `dependency-verificaton.xml` file:
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] ```xml
[codex] [codex] &lt;trusted-keys>
[codex] [codex]    &lt;trusted-key id="7B79ADD11F8A779FE90FD3D0893A028475557671" group="org.gradle" name="github-dependency-graph-gradle-plugin"/>
[codex] [codex] &lt;/trusted-keys>
[codex] [codex] ```
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] ## Using the plugin to generate dependency reports
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] As well as the `GitHubDependencyGraphPlugin`, which is tailored for use by the [gradle/actions/dependency-submission](https://github.com/gradle/actions/tree/main/dependency-submission) GitHub Action, this repository also provides the `SimpleDependencyGraphPlugin`, which generates dependency-graph outputs in simple text format.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] To use the `SimpleDependencyGraphPlugin` you'll need to create an `init.gradle` file to apply the plugin to your project:
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] ```groovy
[codex] [codex] initscript {
[codex] [codex]     repositories {
[codex] [codex]         gradlePluginPortal()
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex]     dependencies {
[codex] [codex]         classpath "org.gradle:github-dependency-graph-gradle-plugin:+"
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] apply plugin: org.gradle.dependencygraph.simple.SimpleDependencyGraphPlugin
[codex] [codex] ```
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] and then execute the task to resolve all dependencies in your project:
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] ```shell
[codex] [codex] ./gradlew -I init.gradle --dependency-verification=off --no-configuration-cache --no-configure-on-demand :ForceDependencyResolutionPlugin_resolveAllDependencies
[codex] [codex] ```
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] You'll find the generated files in `build/reports/dependency-graph-snapshots`.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] ### Using dependency reports to determine the underlying source of a dependency
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] After generating the dependency reports as described, it is possible to determine the dependency source by:
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] 1. Locate the dependency (including matching version) in the `dependency-resolution.json` file.
[codex] [codex] 2. Inspect each `resolvedBy` entry for the `path` and `configuration` values. The `scope` value is unimportant in this context.
[codex] [codex] 3. Use the built-in [dependencyInsight](https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/viewing_debugging_dependencies.html#dependency_insights) task to determine exactly how the dependency was resolved. The `path` indicates the project where the task should be executed, and the `configuration` is an input to the task.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] For example, given the following from the `dependency-resolution.json` report:
[codex] [codex] ```json
[codex] [codex]   "dependency" : "com.google.guava:guava:32.1.3-jre",
[codex] [codex]   "effectiveScope" : "Unknown",
[codex] [codex]   "resolvedBy" : [ {
[codex] [codex]     "path" : ":my-subproject",
[codex] [codex]     "configuration" : "compileClasspath",
[codex] [codex]     "scope" : "Unknown"
[codex] [codex]   }, ...
[codex] [codex] ```
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] You would run the command:
[codex] [codex] ```shell
[codex] [codex] ./gradlew :my-subproject:dependencyInsight --configuration compileClasspath --dependency com.google.guava:guava:32.1.3-jre
[codex] [codex] ```
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] #### Dealing with 'classpath' configuration
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] If the configuration value in `dependency-resolution.json` is "classpath", or for some other reason the above instructions do not work,
[codex] [codex] it is possible to recostruct the full resolution path using the generated `dependency-graph.json` file.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] Search for the exact dependency version in `dependency-graph.json`, and you'll see an "id" entry for that dependency as well as one or more
[codex] [codex] "dependencies" entries. By tracing back through the dependencies you can determine the underlying source of the dependency.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] ## Building/Testing
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] To build and test this plugin, run the following task:
[codex] [codex] ```shell
[codex] [codex] ./gradlew check
[codex] [codex] ```
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] To self-test this plugin and generate a dependency graph for this repository, run:
[codex] [codex] ```shell
[codex] [codex] ./plugin-self-test-local
[codex] [codex] ```
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] The generated dependency graph will be submitted to GitHub only if you supply a
[codex] [codex] [GitHub API token](https://docs.github.com/en/authentication/keeping-your-account-and-data-secure/creating-a-personal-access-token)
[codex] [codex] via the environment variable `GITHUB_TOKEN`.

[codex] [codex] import * as setupGradle from '../../setup-gradle'

[codex] [codex]     DevelocityConfig,
[codex] [codex]     CacheConfig,
[codex] [codex]     DependencyGraphConfig,
[codex] [codex]     DependencyGraphOption,
[codex] [codex]     GradleExecutionConfig,
[codex] [codex]     setActionId,
[codex] [codex]     WrapperValidationConfig
[codex] [codex] } from '../../configuration'

[codex] [codex] /**
[codex] [codex]  * The main entry point for the action, called by Github Actions for the step.
[codex] [codex]  */
[codex] [codex] export async function run(): Promise&lt;void> {
[codex] [codex]     try {
[codex] [codex]         setActionId('gradle/actions/dependency-submission')
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]         // Configure Gradle environment (Gradle User Home)
[codex] [codex]         await setupGradle.setup(new CacheConfig(), new DevelocityConfig(), new WrapperValidationConfig())
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]         // Capture the enabled state of dependency-graph
[codex] [codex]         const originallyEnabled = process.env['GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_ENABLED']
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]         // Configure the dependency graph submission
[codex] [codex]         const config = new DependencyGraphConfig()
[codex] [codex]         await dependencyGraph.setup(config)
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]         if (config.getDependencyGraphOption() === DependencyGraphOption.DownloadAndSubmit) {
[codex] [codex]             // No execution to perform
[codex] [codex]             return
[codex] [codex]         }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]         // Only execute if arguments have been provided
[codex] [codex]         const executionConfig = new GradleExecutionConfig()
[codex] [codex]         const taskList = executionConfig.getDependencyResolutionTask()
[codex] [codex]         const additionalArgs = executionConfig.getAdditionalArguments()
[codex] [codex]         const executionArgs = `
[codex] [codex]               -Dorg.gradle.configureondemand=false
[codex] [codex]               -Dorg.gradle.dependency.verification=off
[codex] [codex]               -Dorg.gradle.unsafe.isolated-projects=false
[codex] [codex]               -Dorg.gradle.isolated-projects=false
[codex] [codex]               ${taskList}
[codex] [codex]               ${additionalArgs}
[codex] [codex]         `
[codex] [codex]         const args: string[] = parseArgsStringToArgv(executionArgs)
[codex] [codex]         await gradle.provisionAndMaybeExecute(
[codex] [codex]             executionConfig.getGradleVersion(),
[codex] [codex]             executionConfig.getBuildRootDirectory(),
[codex] [codex]             args
[codex] [codex]         )
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]         await dependencyGraph.complete(config)
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]         // Reset the enabled state of dependency graph
[codex] [codex]         core.exportVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_ENABLED', originallyEnabled)
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]         saveDeprecationState()
[codex] [codex]     } catch (error) {
[codex] [codex]         handleMainActionError(error)
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]     // Explicit process.exit() to prevent waiting for hanging promises.
[codex] [codex]     await forceExit()
[codex] [codex] }

[codex] [codex] const DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_PREFIX = 'dependency-graph_'
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] export async function setup(config: DependencyGraphConfig): Promise&lt;void> {
[codex] [codex]     const option = config.getDependencyGraphOption()
[codex] [codex]     if (option === DependencyGraphOption.Disabled) {
[codex] [codex]         core.exportVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_ENABLED', 'false')
[codex] [codex]         return
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex]     // Download and submit early, for compatability with dependency review.
[codex] [codex]     if (option === DependencyGraphOption.DownloadAndSubmit) {
[codex] [codex]         maybeExportVariable('DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_REPORT_DIR', config.getReportDirectory())
[codex] [codex]         await downloadAndSubmitDependencyGraphs(config)
[codex] [codex]         return
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]     core.info('Enabling dependency graph generation')
[codex] [codex]     core.exportVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_ENABLED', 'true')
[codex] [codex]     maybeExportVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_CONTINUE_ON_FAILURE', config.getDependencyGraphContinueOnFailure())
[codex] [codex]     maybeExportVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_JOB_CORRELATOR', config.getJobCorrelator())
[codex] [codex]     maybeExportVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_JOB_ID', github.context.runId.toString())
[codex] [codex]     maybeExportVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_REF', github.context.ref)
[codex] [codex]     maybeExportVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_SHA', getShaFromContext())
[codex] [codex]     maybeExportVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_WORKSPACE', getWorkspaceDirectory())
[codex] [codex]     maybeExportVariable('DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_REPORT_DIR', config.getReportDirectory())
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]     maybeExportVariable('DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_EXCLUDE_PROJECTS', config.getExcludeProjects())
[codex] [codex]     maybeExportVariable('DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_INCLUDE_PROJECTS', config.getIncludeProjects())
[codex] [codex]     maybeExportVariable('DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_EXCLUDE_CONFIGURATIONS', config.getExcludeConfigurations())
[codex] [codex]     maybeExportVariable('DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_INCLUDE_CONFIGURATIONS', config.getIncludeConfigurations())
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]     maybeExportVariable('GRADLE_PLUGIN_REPOSITORY_URL', config.getPluginRepository().getUrl())
[codex] [codex]     maybeExportVariable('GRADLE_PLUGIN_REPOSITORY_USERNAME', config.getPluginRepository().getUsername())
[codex] [codex]     maybeExportVariable('GRADLE_PLUGIN_REPOSITORY_PASSWORD', config.getPluginRepository().getPassword())
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] function maybeExportVariable(variableName: string, value: string | boolean | undefined): void {
[codex] [codex]     if (!process.env[variableName]) {
[codex] [codex]         if (value !== undefined) {
[codex] [codex]             core.exportVariable(variableName, value)
[codex] [codex]         }
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] export async function complete(config: DependencyGraphConfig): Promise&lt;void> {
[codex] [codex]     const option = config.getDependencyGraphOption()
[codex] [codex]     try {
[codex] [codex]         switch (option) {
[codex] [codex]             case DependencyGraphOption.Disabled:
[codex] [codex]             case DependencyGraphOption.Generate: // Performed via init-script: nothing to do here
[codex] [codex]             case DependencyGraphOption.DownloadAndSubmit: // Performed in setup
[codex] [codex]                 return
[codex] [codex]             case DependencyGraphOption.GenerateAndSubmit:
[codex] [codex]                 await findAndSubmitDependencyGraphs(config, false)
[codex] [codex]                 return
[codex] [codex]             case DependencyGraphOption.GenerateSubmitAndUpload:
[codex] [codex]                 await findAndSubmitDependencyGraphs(config, true)
[codex] [codex]                 return
[codex] [codex]             case DependencyGraphOption.GenerateAndUpload:
[codex] [codex]                 await findAndUploadDependencyGraphs(config)
[codex] [codex]         }
[codex] [codex]     } catch (e) {
[codex] [codex]         warnOrFail(config, option, e)
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] async function downloadAndSubmitDependencyGraphs(config: DependencyGraphConfig): Promise&lt;void> {
[codex] [codex]     if (isRunningInActEnvironment()) {
[codex] [codex]         core.info('Dependency graph not supported in the ACT environment.')
[codex] [codex]         return
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]     try {
[codex] [codex]         await submitDependencyGraphs(await downloadDependencyGraphs(config))
[codex] [codex]     } catch (e) {
[codex] [codex]         warnOrFail(config, DependencyGraphOption.DownloadAndSubmit, e)
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] async function findAndSubmitDependencyGraphs(config: DependencyGraphConfig, uploadAfterSubmit: boolean): Promise&lt;void> {
[codex] [codex]     if (isRunningInActEnvironment()) {
[codex] [codex]         core.info('Dependency graph not supported in the ACT environment.')
[codex] [codex]         return
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]     const dependencyGraphFiles = await findDependencyGraphFiles()
[codex] [codex]     try {
[codex] [codex]         await submitDependencyGraphs(dependencyGraphFiles)
[codex] [codex]     } catch (e) {
[codex] [codex]         try {
[codex] [codex]             await uploadDependencyGraphs(dependencyGraphFiles, config)
[codex] [codex]         } catch (uploadError) {
[codex] [codex]             core.info(String(uploadError))
[codex] [codex]         }
[codex] [codex]         throw e
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]     if (uploadAfterSubmit) {
[codex] [codex]         await uploadDependencyGraphs(dependencyGraphFiles, config)
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] async function findAndUploadDependencyGraphs(config: DependencyGraphConfig): Promise&lt;void> {
[codex] [codex]     if (isRunningInActEnvironment()) {
[codex] [codex]         core.info('Dependency graph not supported in the ACT environment.')
[codex] [codex]         return
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]     await uploadDependencyGraphs(await findDependencyGraphFiles(), config)
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] async function downloadDependencyGraphs(config: DependencyGraphConfig): Promise&lt;string[]> {
[codex] [codex]     const findBy = github.context.payload.workflow_run
[codex] [codex]         ? {
[codex] [codex]               token: getGithubToken(),
[codex] [codex]               workflowRunId: github.context.payload.workflow_run.id,
[codex] [codex]               repositoryName: github.context.repo.repo,
[codex] [codex]               repositoryOwner: github.context.repo.owner
[codex] [codex]           }
[codex] [codex]         : undefined
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]     const artifactClient = new DefaultArtifactClient()
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]     let dependencyGraphArtifacts = (
[codex] [codex]         await artifactClient.listArtifacts({
[codex] [codex]             latest: true,
[codex] [codex]             findBy
[codex] [codex]         })
[codex] [codex]     ).artifacts.filter(artifact => artifact.name.startsWith(DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_PREFIX))
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]     const artifactName = config.getDownloadArtifactName()
[codex] [codex]     if (artifactName) {
[codex] [codex]         core.info(`Filtering for artifacts ending with ${artifactName}`)
[codex] [codex]         dependencyGraphArtifacts = dependencyGraphArtifacts.filter(artifact => artifact.name.includes(artifactName))
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]     for (const artifact of dependencyGraphArtifacts) {
[codex] [codex]         const downloadedArtifact = await artifactClient.downloadArtifact(artifact.id, {
[codex] [codex]             findBy
[codex] [codex]         })
[codex] [codex]         core.info(`Downloading dependency-graph artifact ${artifact.name} to ${downloadedArtifact.downloadPath}`)
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]     return findDependencyGraphFiles()
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] async function findDependencyGraphFiles(): Promise&lt;string[]> {
[codex] [codex]     const globber = await glob.create(`${getReportDirectory()}/**/*.json`)
[codex] [codex]     const allFiles = await globber.glob()
[codex] [codex]     const unprocessedFiles = allFiles.filter(file => !isProcessed(file))
[codex] [codex]     unprocessedFiles.forEach(markProcessed)
[codex] [codex]     core.info(`Found dependency graph files: ${unprocessedFiles.join(', ')}`)
[codex] [codex]     return unprocessedFiles
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] async function uploadDependencyGraphs(dependencyGraphFiles: string[], config: DependencyGraphConfig): Promise&lt;void> {
[codex] [codex]     if (dependencyGraphFiles.length === 0) {
[codex] [codex]         core.info('No dependency graph files found to upload.')
[codex] [codex]         return
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]     const workspaceDirectory = getWorkspaceDirectory()
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]     const artifactClient = new DefaultArtifactClient()
[codex] [codex]     for (const dependencyGraphFile of dependencyGraphFiles) {
[codex] [codex]         const relativePath = getRelativePathFromWorkspace(dependencyGraphFile)
[codex] [codex]         core.info(`Uploading dependency graph file: ${relativePath}`)
[codex] [codex]         const artifactName = `${DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_PREFIX}${path.basename(dependencyGraphFile)}`
[codex] [codex]         await artifactClient.uploadArtifact(artifactName, [dependencyGraphFile], workspaceDirectory, {
[codex] [codex]             retentionDays: config.getArtifactRetentionDays()
[codex] [codex]         })
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] async function submitDependencyGraphs(dependencyGraphFiles: string[]): Promise&lt;void> {
[codex] [codex]     if (dependencyGraphFiles.length === 0) {
[codex] [codex]         core.info('No dependency graph files found to submit.')
[codex] [codex]         return
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]     for (const dependencyGraphFile of dependencyGraphFiles) {
[codex] [codex]         try {
[codex] [codex]             await submitDependencyGraphFile(dependencyGraphFile)
[codex] [codex]         } catch (error) {
[codex] [codex]             if (error instanceof Error && error.name === 'HttpError') {
[codex] [codex]                 error.message = translateErrorMessage(dependencyGraphFile, error)
[codex] [codex]             }
[codex] [codex]             throw error
[codex] [codex]         }
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] function translateErrorMessage(jsonFile: string, error: Error): string {
[codex] [codex]     const relativeJsonFile = getRelativePathFromWorkspace(jsonFile)
[codex] [codex]     const mainWarning = `Dependency submission failed for ${relativeJsonFile}.\n${error.message}`
[codex] [codex]     if (error.message === 'Resource not accessible by integration') {
[codex] [codex]         return `${mainWarning}
[codex] [codex] Please ensure that the 'contents: write' permission is available for the workflow job.
[codex] [codex] Note that this permission is never available for a 'pull_request' trigger from a repository fork.
[codex] [codex]         `
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex]     return mainWarning
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] async function submitDependencyGraphFile(jsonFile: string): Promise&lt;void> {
[codex] [codex]     const octokit = getOctokit()
[codex] [codex]     const jsonContent = fs.readFileSync(jsonFile, 'utf8')
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]     const jsonObject = JSON.parse(jsonContent)
[codex] [codex]     jsonObject.owner = github.context.repo.owner
[codex] [codex]     jsonObject.repo = github.context.repo.repo
[codex] [codex]     const response = await octokit.request('POST /repos/{owner}/{repo}/dependency-graph/snapshots', jsonObject)
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]     const relativeJsonFile = getRelativePathFromWorkspace(jsonFile)
[codex] [codex]     core.notice(`Submitted ${relativeJsonFile}: ${response.data.message}`)
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] function getReportDirectory(): string {
[codex] [codex]     return process.env.DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_REPORT_DIR!
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] function isProcessed(dependencyGraphFile: string): boolean {
[codex] [codex]     const markerFile = `${dependencyGraphFile}.processed`
[codex] [codex]     return fs.existsSync(markerFile)
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] function markProcessed(dependencyGraphFile: string): void {
[codex] [codex]     const markerFile = `${dependencyGraphFile}.processed`
[codex] [codex]     fs.writeFileSync(markerFile, '')
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] function warnOrFail(config: DependencyGraphConfig, option: String, error: unknown): void {
[codex] [codex]     if (!config.getDependencyGraphContinueOnFailure()) {
[codex] [codex]         throw new JobFailure(error)
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]     core.warning(`Failed to ${option} dependency graph. Will continue.\n${String(error)}`)
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] function getOctokit(): InstanceType&lt;typeof GitHub> {
[codex] [codex]     return github.getOctokit(getGithubToken())
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] function getRelativePathFromWorkspace(file: string): string {
[codex] [codex]     const workspaceDirectory = getWorkspaceDirectory()
[codex] [codex]     return path.relative(workspaceDirectory, file)
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] function getShaFromContext(): string {
[codex] [codex]     const context = github.context
[codex] [codex]     const pullRequestEvents = [
[codex] [codex]         'pull_request',
[codex] [codex]         'pull_request_comment',
[codex] [codex]         'pull_request_review',
[codex] [codex]         'pull_request_review_comment'
[codex] [codex]         // Note that pull_request_target is omitted here.
[codex] [codex]         // That event runs in the context of the base commit of the PR,
[codex] [codex]         // so the snapshot should not be associated with the head commit.
[codex] [codex]     ]
[codex] [codex]     if (pullRequestEvents.includes(context.eventName)) {
[codex] [codex]         const pr = (context.payload as PullRequestEvent).pull_request
[codex] [codex]         return pr.head.sha
[codex] [codex]     } else {
[codex] [codex]         return context.sha
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] function isRunningInActEnvironment(): boolean {
[codex] [codex]     return process.env.ACT !== undefined
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] import org.gradle.util.GradleVersion
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] // Only run when dependency graph is explicitly enabled
[codex] [codex] if (getVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_ENABLED') != "true") {
[codex] [codex]   return
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] // Do not run for unsupported versions of Gradle
[codex] [codex] def gradleVersion = GradleVersion.current().baseVersion
[codex] [codex] if (gradleVersion &lt; GradleVersion.version("5.2") ||
[codex] [codex]     (gradleVersion >= GradleVersion.version("7.0") && gradleVersion &lt; GradleVersion.version("7.1"))) {
[codex] [codex]   if (getVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_CONTINUE_ON_FAILURE') != "true") {
[codex] [codex]     throw new GradleException("Dependency Graph is not supported for ${gradleVersion}. No dependency snapshot will be generated.")
[codex] [codex]   }
[codex] [codex]   logger.warn("::warning::Dependency Graph is not supported for ${gradleVersion}. No dependency snapshot will be generated.")
[codex] [codex]   return
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] // Attempt to find a unique job correlator to use based on the environment variable
[codex] [codex] // This is only required for top-level builds
[codex] [codex] def isTopLevelBuild = gradle.getParent() == null
[codex] [codex] if (isTopLevelBuild) {
[codex] [codex]   def reportFile = getUniqueReportFile(getVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_JOB_CORRELATOR'))
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]   if (reportFile == null) {
[codex] [codex]     logger.warn("::warning::No dependency snapshot generated for step. Could not determine unique job correlator - specify GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_JOB_CORRELATOR var for this step.")
[codex] [codex]     return
[codex] [codex]   }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]   logger.lifecycle("Generating dependency graph into '${reportFile}'")
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] apply from: 'gradle-actions.github-dependency-graph-gradle-plugin-apply.groovy'
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] /**
[codex] [codex]  * Using the supplied jobCorrelator value:
[codex] [codex]  * - Checks if report file already exists
[codex] [codex]  * - If so, tries to find a unique value that does not yet have a corresponding report file.
[codex] [codex]  * - When found, this value is set as a System property override.
[codex] [codex]  */
[codex] [codex] File getUniqueReportFile(String jobCorrelator) {
[codex] [codex]     def reportDir = getVariable('DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_REPORT_DIR')
[codex] [codex]     def reportFile = new File(reportDir, jobCorrelator + ".json")
[codex] [codex]     if (!reportFile.exists()) return reportFile
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]     // Try at most 100 suffixes
[codex] [codex]     for (int i = 1; i &lt; 100; i++) {
[codex] [codex]         def candidateCorrelator = jobCorrelator + "-" + i
[codex] [codex]         def candidateFile = new File(reportDir, candidateCorrelator + ".json")
[codex] [codex]         if (!candidateFile.exists()) {
[codex] [codex]            System.properties['GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_JOB_CORRELATOR'] = candidateCorrelator
[codex] [codex]            return candidateFile
[codex] [codex]         }
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]     // Could not determine unique job correlator
[codex] [codex]     return null
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] /**
[codex] [codex] * Return the environment variable value, or equivalent system property (if set)
[codex] [codex] */
[codex] [codex] String getVariable(String name) {
[codex] [codex]   return System.properties[name] ?: System.getenv(name)
[codex] [codex] }
[codex] [codex] buildscript {
[codex] [codex]   def getInputParam = { String name ->
[codex] [codex]       def envVarName = name.toUpperCase().replace('.', '_').replace('-', '_')
[codex] [codex]       return System.getProperty(name) ?: System.getenv(envVarName)
[codex] [codex]   }
[codex] [codex]   def pluginRepositoryUrl = getInputParam('gradle.plugin-repository.url') ?: 'https://plugins.gradle.org/m2'
[codex] [codex]   def pluginRepositoryUsername = getInputParam('gradle.plugin-repository.username')
[codex] [codex]   def pluginRepositoryPassword = getInputParam('gradle.plugin-repository.password')
[codex] [codex]   def dependencyGraphPluginVersion = getInputParam('dependency-graph-plugin.version') ?: '1.4.2'
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex]   logger.lifecycle("Resolving dependency graph plugin ${dependencyGraphPluginVersion} from plugin repository: ${pluginRepositoryUrl}")
[codex] [codex]   repositories {
[codex] [codex]     maven { 
[codex] [codex]       url = pluginRepositoryUrl
[codex] [codex]       if (pluginRepositoryUsername && pluginRepositoryPassword) {
[codex] [codex]         logger.lifecycle("Applying credentials for plugin repository: ${pluginRepositoryUrl}")
[codex] [codex]         credentials {
[codex] [codex]           username = pluginRepositoryUsername
[codex] [codex]           password = pluginRepositoryPassword
[codex] [codex]         }
[codex] [codex]         authentication {
[codex] [codex]           basic(BasicAuthentication)
[codex] [codex]         }
[codex] [codex]       }
[codex] [codex]     }
[codex] [codex]   }
[codex] [codex]   dependencies {
[codex] [codex]     classpath "org.gradle:github-dependency-graph-gradle-plugin:${dependencyGraphPluginVersion}"
[codex] [codex]   }
[codex] [codex] }
